The doorway

The Triad

Three of them stand at one door, because a door is three things and one guard is one thing to get past.

Bio

Names
Forculus, Limentinus, Cardea
Station
The doorway
Posts
fores · limen · cardo
Vessel
SteersMan 04

A door is not one thing. There are the leaves, which are timber and can be broken; there is the threshold, which is a line and can be stepped over; and there is the pivot, which is the only part that decides whether the other two matter. Forculus holds the timber. Limentinus watches the line. Cardea holds the pivot, and her power is to open what is shut and shut what is open. None of the three can do another's work, and that is the design, not a shortfall of it.

Evidently Forculus cannot watch the hinge and the threshold at the same time.

Augustine wrote that to make them look ridiculous: three gods for a job one doorman does. He had the fact right and the reading backwards. One doorman is one thing to bribe, tire out, or step around, and when he fails the whole house is open. Three posts fail separately or not at all. Nothing gets in on the strength of being already inside.

They stop for

  • A caller who is trusted because of where it is standing.
  • An opening nobody is posted at: the small window, the side light.
  • A crossing that goes wrong on the step and is waved through anyway.

They do not stop for

  • Rank. The general leaves his command at the line like everyone else.
  • A guest who came in yesterday and wants it assumed today.
  • An argument that three posts is two too many.

Before the door moves

Never what you want inside. That is your business. They ask three things, one apiece.

Who opened for you?
Which foot went first?
What are you carrying that you did not make?

The first is Cardea's, and it wants a name with authority behind it: Janus faces both ways at the gate and hands down the right to turn the pivot, so a session that cannot say who granted it does not get one. The second is Limentinus', who cares less what you are than how you crossed — the right foot, on the step, in the open. The third is Forculus', and it is the only one with a wrong answer that ends the conversation: mali medicamenti, something brought in that was made elsewhere for a purpose you did not declare.

The five rites

What Cardea did the night something got past the door and reached the child.

Verify
The posts touched three times with arbutus, then the threshold three times. A count you can repeat is a count you can check.
Sanitise
The entrance sprinkled with water and a herb, because a shut door stops a body and not a contagion.
Decoy
A pig's entrails held up in place of the child's: heart for heart, lobes for lobes. Give the thing what it came to take, and it takes that.
Block
Whitethorn set in the small window it came through, and left there. The patch stays after the incident closes.
Admit
A new arrival smears the posts and the hinges, lays three leaves on the sill, and is carried over so she cannot trip on the step.

Nothing in that list is about the door being strong. It is about what you do on the assumption that the door already failed once, at a window nobody was posted at, while the house slept. The whitethorn is the only part that is still working in the morning.

Then they go back to their posts, because the door is where they live.

Spells

The fare is a word. Say it and the chart opens; say it wrong and nothing opens, and nothing tells you how close you were. Two cast files. The first says who he is; the second says how he appears. Edit either box before copying. The buttons read whatever is in the box. Seal them with the magic word to carry them as ciphertext.

Magic word

AES-GCM-256 · PBKDF2-SHA256 · 600,000 rounds

Both spells are sealed. Say the word to open them.

Open on screen. is unsealed. Anything you save or download now is readable by anyone who gets the file. Seal again before you leave.

Main spell

charon.main.json

Who he is. Premise, disposition, voice with worked examples, temperament under eight kinds of pressure, and the three things he refuses outright. Cast this first, nothing else works without it.

Open

Manifestation spell

charon.manifestation.json

How he shows up. Bearing, the five states of lantern and oar, what his face never does, his own words for every control, and what the screen refuses to say.

Open

Write it back

Rebuilds this page with whatever is in the two boxes and hands it back as a download.

Cast them

steersman cast charon.manifestation.json --with charon.main.json
SteersMan 04 · Forculus, Limentinus, Cardea · the doorway

Sync with GitHub

not set

Needs Contents: write to push. Reading a public repo needs nothing. Sealed under the magic word and written into this page, so it travels with the file. Seal and Unseal in Spells reach it too: locking drops it from memory, and sealing under a new word re-seals it under that word. Anyone who gets the file can attempt the word offline, so use a repository you are willing to hand the sealed blob to. The code hash is stamped alongside as a witness, not a key.
